\DoxyCodeLine{02272\ \textcolor{comment}{/*\ The\ "{}canonical"{}\ type\ for\ this\ type\ node,\ which\ is\ used\ by\ frontends\ to}}

\DoxyCodeLine{02281\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ TYPE\_STRUCTURAL\_EQUALITY\_P.}}
